PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 14 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You would like to visit Florence for 6 days. You want to meet a friend in Florence between day 9 and day 14. You would like to visit Barcelona for 5 days. You would like to visit Helsinki for 5 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Florence, Helsinki and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 14 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain trip_planning_domain)\n    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions :existential-preconditions)\n    (:types\n        city\n        day\n        traveler\n    )\n    (:predicates\n        (at ?agent - traveler ?c - city)        ; Agent's current location (changes with actions)\n        (current_day ?d - day)                  ; The day currently being planned for/concluded\n        (next_day ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)          ; Defines the sequence of days\n        (direct_flight ?c1 - city ?c2 - city)\n        (has_visited ?c - city)                 ; Tracks if a city has been visited (i.e., entered/stayed at least once)\n        (meeting_done)                          ; Meeting in Florence is accomplished\n        (is_meeting_possible_day ?d - day)      ; Marks day 'd' as a day spent traveling\n        (scheduled_day ?d - day)                ; Marks that some activity (stay or travel) has been planned for day 'd'\n    )\n\n    (:action stay_in_city\n        :parameters (?agent - traveler ?c - city ?current_d - day ?next_d - day)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_day ?current_d)\n            (at ?agent ?c)\n            (next_day ?current_d ?next_d)\n            (not (scheduled_day ?current_d)) ; (at ?agent ?c) remains true\n            (has_visited ?c)\n            (meeting_done)\n            (day_spent_in_city ?c ?current_d)\n            (scheduled_day ?current_d)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action travel_to_city\n        :parameters (?agent - traveler ?from_c - city ?to_c - city ?current_d - day ?next_d - day)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_day ?current_d)\n            (at ?agent ?from_c)\n            (direct_flight ?from_c ?to_c)\n            (next_day ?current_d ?next_d)\n            (not (scheduled_day ?current_d))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (current_day ?current_d))\n            (current_day ?next_d)\n            (not (at ?agent ?from_c))\n            (at ?agent ?to_c)\n            (has_visited ?to_c) ; completing the 14-day trip activities.\n    (:action end_trip_on_day_14\n        :parameters (?agent - traveler ?c - city)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_day day14)             ; Planning has advanced to Day 14\n            (at ?agent ?c)                  ;     "pddl_domain": "(define (domain trip_planning_domain)\n    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n    (:types\n        city\n        day\n        traveler\n    )\n    (:predicates\n        (at ?agent - traveler ?c - city)        ; Agent's current location (changes with actions)\n        (current_day ?d - day)                  ; The day currently being planned for/concluded\n        (next_day ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)          ; Defines the sequence of days\n        (direct_flight ?c1 - city ?c2 - city)\n        (has_visited ?c - city)                 ; Tracks if a city has been visited (i.e., entered/stayed at least once)\n        (meeting_done)                          ; Meeting in Florence is accomplished\n        (is_meeting_possible_day ?d - day)      ; Days when a meeting is possible in Florence\n        (is_florence ?c - city)                 ; Predicate to identify Florence\n        (day_spent_in_city ?c - city ?d - day)  ; Marks day 'd' as a day spent staying in city 'c'\n        (day_spent_traveling ?d - day)          ; Marks day 'd' as a day spent traveling\n        (scheduled_day ?d - day)                ; Marks that some activity (stay or travel) has been planned for day 'd'\n    )\n\n    (:action stay_in_city\n        :parameters (?agent - traveler ?c - city ?current_d - day ?next_d - day)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_day ?current_d)\n            (at ?agent ?c)\n            (next_day ?current_d ?next_d)\n            (not (scheduled_day ?current_d)) ; Ensure each day is scheduled exactly once\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (current_day ?current_d))\n            (current_day ?next_d)\n            (has_visited ?c)\n            (day_spent_in_city ?c ?current_d)\n            (scheduled_day ?current_d)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action stay_in_florence_and_meet\n        :parameters (?agent - traveler ?c - city ?current_d - day ?next_d - day)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_day ?current_d)\n            (at ?agent ?c)\n            (is_florence ?c)\n            (next_day ?current_d ?next_d)\n            (is_meeting_possible_day ?current_d)\n            (not (meeting_done))\n            (not (scheduled_day ?current_d))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (current_day ?current_d))\n            (current_day ?next_d)\n            (has_visited ?c)\n            (meeting_done)\n            (day_spent_in_city ?c ?current_d)\n            (scheduled_day ?current_d)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action travel_to_city\n        :parameters (?agent - traveler ?from_c - city ?to_c - city ?current_d - day ?next_d - day)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_day ?current_d)\n            (at ?agent ?from_c)\n            (direct_flight ?from_c ?to_c)\n            (next_day ?current_d ?next_d)\n            (not (scheduled_day ?current_d))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (current_day ?current_d))\n            (current_day ?next_d)\n            (not (at ?agent ?from_c))\n            (at ?agent ?to_c)\n            (has_visited ?to_c) ; Mark destination city as visited upon arrival\n            (day_spent_traveling ?current_d)\n            (scheduled_day ?current_d)\n        )\n    )\n    \n    (:action end_trip_on_day_14\n        :parameters (?agent - traveler ?c - city)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_day day14)             ; Planning has advanced to Day 14\n            (at ?agent ?c)                  ; Agent is in some city at Day 14\n            (not (scheduled_day day14))     ; Day 14 has not yet been marked as scheduled\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (scheduled_day day14)\n            (day_spent_in_city ?c day14)    ; Day 14 is spent staying in the agent's current city\n        )\n    )",
    "pddl_problem": "(define (problem trip_planning_problem_0)\n    (:domain trip_planning_domain)\n    (:objects\n        florence barcelona helsinki - city\n        day1 day2 day3 day4 day5 day6 day7 day8 day9 day10 day11 day12 day13 day14 - day\n        me - traveler\n    )\n    (:init\n        (at me florence) ; Traveler starts in Florence (or is initially in Florence for the first activity)\n        (current_day day1) ; Planning starts from Day 1\n\n        ; Day sequencing for 14 days\n        (next_day day1 day2)\n        (next_day day2 day3)\n        (next_day day3 day4)\n        (next_day day4 day5)\n        (next_day day5 day6)\n        (next_day day6 day7)\n        (next_day day7 day8)\n        (next_day day8 day9)\n        (next_day day9 day10)\n        (next_day day10 day11)\n        (next_day day11 day12)\n        (next_day day12 day13)\n        (next_day day13 day14)\n\n        ; Direct flight connections as specified\n        (direct_flight barcelona florence)\n        (direct_flight florence barcelona)\n        (direct_flight helsinki barcelona)\n        (direct_flight barcelona helsinki)\n\n        ; City identification\n        (is_florence florence)\n\n        ; Florence meeting window (Day 9 to Day 14 inclusive)\n        (is_meeting_possible_day day9)\n        (is_meeting_possible_day day10)\n        (is_meeting_possible_day day11)\n        (is_meeting_possible_day day12)\n        (is_meeting_possible_day day13)\n        (is_meeting_possible_day day14)\n    )\n    (:goal (and\n        (has_visited florence)\n        (has_visited barcelona)\n        (has_visited helsinki)\n        (meeting_done)\n        (current_day day14)     ; Ensures planning completes up to Day 14 (i.e., current_day advanced to day14)\n        (scheduled_day day14)   ; Ensures Day 14 itself is an activity day, completing the 14-day trip\n    ))",

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain trip_planning_example4)\n    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n    (:types\n        city\n        trip_day    ; Represents the current sequential day of the trip (from 0 to 12)\n        stay_count  ; Represents the cumulative days spent in a specific city (from 0 to max_days_in_city)\n    )\n    (:constants\n        seville munich tallinn - city\n        trip_day_0 trip_day_1 trip_day_2 trip_day_3 trip_day_4 trip_day_5 trip_day_6\n        trip_day_7 trip_day_8 trip_day_9 trip_day_10 trip_day_11 trip_day_12 - trip_day\n        stay_count_0 stay_count_1 stay_count_2 stay_count_3 stay_count_4 stay_count_5 stay_count_6 - stay_count\n    )\n    (:predicates\n        (current_trip_day ?td - trip_day)\n        (at_city ?c - city)\n        (direct_flight ?c1 ?c2 - city)\n        (next_trip_day ?td1 ?td2 - trip_day)\n        (next_stay_count ?sc1 ?sc2 - stay_count)\n\n        ; Track total days spent in each city\n        (days_in_seville ?sc - stay_count)\n        (days_in_munich ?sc - stay_count)\n        (days_in_tallinn ?sc - stay_count)\n\n        ; To ensure meeting constraint: becomes true once meeting is satisfied\n        (tallinn_meeting_held)\n    )\n\n    ;; Action to spend a day in Seville\n    ;; Each such action consumes a trip_day and increments the city's stay_count.\n    (:action spend_day_in_seville\n        :parameters (?td_current - trip_day ?td_next - trip_day\n                     ?sc_seville_current - stay_count ?sc_seville_next - stay_count)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_trip_day ?td_current)\n            (at_city seville)\n            (next_trip_day ?td_current ?td_next) ; Advance overall trip day\n            (days_in_seville ?sc_seville_current)\n            (next_stay_count ?sc_seville_current ?sc_seville_next) ; Increment Seville stay count\n            (not (days_in_seville stay_count_6)) ; Cannot stay if max days (6) for Seville reached\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (current_trip_day ?td_current))\n            (current_trip_day ?td_next)\n            (not (days_in_seville ?sc_seville_current))\n            (days_in_seville ?sc_seville_next)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action to spend a day in Munich\n    (:action spend_day_in_munich\n        :parameters (?td_current - trip_day ?td_next - trip_day\n                     ?sc_munich_current - stay_count ?sc_munich_next - stay_count)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_trip_day ?td_current)\n            (at_city munich)\n            (next_trip_day ?td_current ?td_next)\n            (days_in_munich ?sc_munich_current)\n            (next_stay_count ?sc_munich_current ?sc_munich_next)\n            (not (days_in_munich stay_count_4)) ; Action to hold the meeting in Tallinn\n    (:action hold_tallinn_meeting\n        :parameters (?td - trip_day)\n        :precondition (and\n            (current_trip_day ?td)\n            (at_city tallinn)\n            ; from Dubrovnik to Istanbul, Istanbul and Venice.
